- **requirement-analysis**: 需求分析（阶段 0）。在方案设计前，用苏格拉底式诘问和第一性原理深挖需求背后的根源动机与痛点本质，质疑需求真伪，逼策划举证论证。默认立场是怀疑——只有策划能论证需求值得做，才放行进入 brainstorming。采用四层诘问：去伪（需求真伪）→ 归因（根源动机）→ 痛点深挖（场景/类型/绕过成本/强度/反向证伪）→ 必要性（值得现在做）。举证制：AI 施加举证压力，但策划可强制放行（显式记录）。 ([NEGU30/requirement-to-development](https://github.com/NEGU30/requirement-to-development/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/requirement-analysis))

([jhon2397/proyecto-loop](https://github.com/jhon2397/proyecto-loop/tree/HEAD/skills/requirements-analysis))
- **requirements-analysis**: Review, clarify, and enrich product requirements for BA sign-off and LSE readiness. Triggers on: analyze requirements, BA review, clarify PRD, review product requirements, enrich requirements, create ba-analysis, prepare PRD v2. ([jscoobyced/jsc-agent-fleet](https://github.com/jscoobyced/jsc-agent-fleet/tree/HEAD/skills/requirements-analysis))
- **requirements-analysis**: Turn a vague request into clear requirements, user stories and testable acceptance criteria - use for product/feature definition and scoping. ([kashvats/p_assistant](https://github.com/kashvats/p_assistant/tree/HEAD/src/living_assistant/builtin_skills/requirements-analysis))
- **requirements-analysis**: 面向软件、AI、模型、算法、算子和平台项目生成或修订开发前视角的完整需求分析报告。用于识别客户真实需求、拆解需求组成、调研并选择技术方案、评估技术/数据/资源/交付/运营/合规可行性、估算工作量、判断是否值得做、定义需求与验收标准、确定优先级和建立需求基线；也适用于用户只提供穿刺需求、已有代码、测试或实验，但要求补齐正式需求分析思路和报告的场景。现有实现只能作为技术调研材料，不得把完成情况、测试结果或上线效果写入需求报告。 ([Kirrito-k423/agent-skills](https://github.com/Kirrito-k423/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/requirements-analysis))
- **requirements-analysis**: Analyze software requirements and transform natural-language requirements into structured functional requirements, non-functional requirements, user stories, acceptance criteria, and traceability information. ([LeBinhcd/Cinema_System](https://github.com/LeBinhcd/Cinema_System/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/requirements-analysis) · 2 copies)
- **requirements-analysis**: 软件需求分析技能包。当需要分析用户需求、输出功能规格、识别核心功能模块时使用。 ([map-A/visual-skill-manager](https://github.com/map-A/visual-skill-manager/tree/HEAD/skills/requirements-analysis))
- **requirements-analysis**: Turn ambiguous feature requests into explicit, testable requirements. Use when a request is incomplete, behavior is ambiguous, scope is non-trivial, or implementation could reasonably differ. ([mehedi0-007/enterprise-agent-skills](https://github.com/mehedi0-007/enterprise-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/engineering/requirements-analysis))
- **requirements-analysis**: Formalizes a customer's informal problem into a requirements record: environment model, goal hierarchy, constraints, and external interfaces. Asks the customer when a gap would make the record wrong. Does not write a functional spec or product code. Use when the user runs /requirements-analysis, asks to analyze requirements, formalize a problem statement, capture needs and constraints, or turn feedback into requirements. ([mkarots/cursor-skills](https://github.com/mkarots/cursor-skills/tree/HEAD/requirements-analysis))
